## v3.983.0 - 2026-07-21

Plan 88 Step 1 — guideline digest for the backend-v1 build. New file `docs/plans/88/guideline-digest.md` distills every applicable rule from `spec/02-coding-guidelines/**`, `spec/02-coding-guidelines/21-app/**`, `spec/14-update/**`, `spec/17-consolidated-guidelines/**`, and `spec/03-error-manage/**` into a single reference the rest of Plan 88 cites: PascalCase Universal Response Envelope, reserved backend error range `BE-4000..BE-4999` (`CAM-1000..1099` for camera facade), no `Any`/`dict` in Python signatures, function <=15 lines, no nested `if`, no `else` after `return`/`raise`, no `fetch(` in `src/**` outside `src/lib/backend/**`, no `sdk/**` imports outside `BE/sdk-facade/**` or `src/lib/sdk-facade/**`, uvicorn bound to `127.0.0.1` in dev with localhost-only CORS, structured JSON logs with `requestId`/`durMs`, `run.ps1`/`run.sh` idempotent with `--be-port`/`--fe-port`/`--no-shell`. No runtime code changed — reference artefact only. Unblocks Step 2 (spec-gap log) and Step 4 (Mermaid diagrams grounded in the real envelope + facade contract).

## v3.982.0 - 2026-07-21

Fixed the two broken UI issues from `assets/ui/70-*`, `71-*`, `72-*`:

- **Giant purple horizontal scrollbar in Layers/Preview/Properties panels**: `panel-chrome-body` was using `overflow-auto` (both axes) with no styled scrollbar, so any minor horizontal overflow surfaced as a chunky OS-native bar. Switched to `overflow-y-auto overflow-x-hidden editor-scroll-fancy` so every dockable panel uses the same thin 8px accent scrollbar and never shows a horizontal scrollbar.
- **Redundant Properties Legend strip**: removed `StatusLegend` from `PropertiesPanel.tsx`. Tone is already conveyed by each card's left stripe (BOUNDS green, ACCEPTANCE amber, etc.) and the Verdict pill in the identity row, so the legend was pure duplication eating ~48px of scarce panel height.

## v3.981.0 - 2026-07-21

- Properties panel rule-section breakage fixed from the screenshot evidence in `assets/issues/19-properties-panel-rules-broken.md`. The broken pieces were horizontal overflow escaping the right rail, Kind editor bodies drawing wider than the panel, Mask helper text and upload controls colliding, nested section padding leaking in from the generic `.editor-shell aside section` rule, and bottom content being hidden behind the status footer. Added explicit Properties scroll containment, card width clamps, nested rail body overflow guards, compact `editor-kind-panel` and `editor-mask-panel` primitives, and a scoped reset so generic aside-section padding no longer affects Bounds / Mask / Kind content.

## v3.980.0 - 2026-07-21

- Rules panel left gutter fixed at the real source: the broad `.editor-shell aside section` rule was applying 16px padding to the `Rules` and `Categories` list sections, so both the sticky section header and every row started about 17px inside the scrollport even after the row badge and drag handle were corrected. Added a scoped override for `section[data-layer-section]` to remove that inherited padding and border, keeping list gutters owned only by `.editor-layer-section-head` and `.editor-rule-row`. Tightened `tests/visual/rules-panel-left-gutter.spec.ts` so its fixture includes the real editor shell/aside/section chain and now asserts section, header, row, and row-main geometry. Fixes `assets/issues/18-rules-panel-left-gutter-persists.md`.

## v3.979.0 - 2026-07-21

- Rules row hover no longer reshuffles the leading edge. The order badge stays locked at its resting position (top:50% / translateY(-50%)) in every state; hover/focus/selected now change only border-color + text color + opacity. Removes the `top: 2px; transform: none` reposition at `src/styles.css:2040-2048` that made the row read as "sliding right / gluing" on hover. Documented in `assets/issues/17-rules-row-hover-shifts-position.md` with the hard rule: hover states may change color/background/opacity/border/box-shadow only, never geometry (top/left/right/bottom, transform translate/scale, padding, margin, width, height). Properties panel breakage from the same user message is deferred to a follow-up issue pending a fresh screenshot of what's broken.

## v3.978.0 - 2026-07-21

- New visual regression spec `tests/visual/rules-panel-left-gutter.spec.ts` locks the Rules panel left gutter in both `comfortable` and `compact` density. Renders a minimal LayerRow fixture via `page.setContent()` (drag handle + order badge + `.editor-rule-row-main`) loading the live `src/styles.css` through the dev server, then (a) asserts the row-main's left edge is within 12px of the row content-left (fails immediately if the drag handle or order badge regresses to in-flow width) and (b) pixel-diffs the captured row against a baseline in `tests/reports/screenshots/plan69/baseline/`. Baseline auto-seeds on first run; refresh with `VISUAL_UPDATE=1 bunx playwright test tests/visual/rules-panel-left-gutter.spec.ts`. Prevents the red-box gutter regression flagged in `assets/issues/16-rules-panel-left-gutter-padding.md` from returning silently.

## v3.977.0 - 2026-07-21

- Execution order badge (`.editor-rule-order-badge`) refactored from an in-flow `flex: 0 0 18px` chip to an absolute-positioned leading overlay pinned to the row's left edge (`left: 1px`, vertically centered). Idle rows show the number at 75% opacity as a compact 12px chip; hover/focus/selection promote it to full opacity and tuck it to the top-left corner so it does not collide with the fading-in drag handle. Row-main (kind icon + name) now anchors to the row's padding-left regardless of badge presence, so rule content aligns with the section header text and no longer drifts right by ~21px.

## v3.976.0 - 2026-07-21

- Rule row drag handle refactored to a pure overlay: `[data-layer-drag-handle]` is now `position: absolute` at `left: 1px`, hidden by default (`opacity: 0`, `pointer-events: none`), and fades in on row hover / focus / selection. Handle no longer participates in the flex layout, so it cannot reserve width or shift content right when the row is idle. Removes the last source of left-gutter drift in the Rules panel.

## v3.975.0 - 2026-07-21

- Rules panel compact-density left gutter fix: the `.right-rail[data-density="compact"] .editor-rule-row` override was stamping `padding: 2px 5px 2px 12px`, which overrode the base row's 6px left padding and reintroduced the empty column at the left edge of the Rules list whenever density was Compact (the default on the right rail). Dropped `padding-left` to 4px so compact rows align with the tab-strip / section-head gutter and match the base row grid. Fixes `assets/issues/16-rules-panel-left-gutter-padding.md` (screenshot: `assets/ui/64-rules-panel-left-gutter-still-present.png`).

## v3.970.0 - 2026-07-20

- Layers panel left gutter fix: `.editor-rule-row` padding-left dropped 12px -> 6px and drag handle now idles at 6px width / 35% opacity, expanding to 16px on hover/focus/selection. Rule rows align flush with the `RULES` / `CATEGORIES` section headers, eliminating the wide empty column on the left side of the panel highlighted in `assets/issues/13-layers-panel-left-gutter-gap.png`. Selection accent stripe repositioned to `left:1px`.

## v3.968.0 - 2026-07-20

- Layers panel section headers ("RULES" / "CATEGORIES") left padding reduced 12px -> 6px so the label sits flush with the tab-strip gutter instead of appearing indented. Also queued 4 header redesign proposals for user selection (see `assets/issues/10-rules-section-header-visualization.md`).

## v3.967.0 - 2026-07-20

- Top-bar Publish is now the user-selected v4 "Preview + Publish Duo": a segmented pill hosts a leading Preview action (eye icon + label), a hairline divider, then the primary Publish button and its caret dropdown (Publish / Draft / Schedule / Copy live URL / Rollback). Adds `.editor-topbar-preview` styling so the leading segment reads as a secondary control while Publish keeps primary emphasis.

## v3.966.0 - 2026-07-20

- Properties panel: removed the Accept / Reject / Ignore verdict bar. Verdict is expressed through Acceptance conditions themselves (Must be present / Must be absent / Ignore), so users add and stack conditions from one place instead of choosing between a top-level verdict and a per-condition presence mode. Reclaims ~40px of chrome and eliminates the two-source confusion flagged in the latest UI review.

## v3.965.0 - 2026-07-20

- Properties floating panel header: collapsed the two-band chrome (title row + segmented pill tabs) into a single 32px titlebar. Tabs (Preview / Layers / Properties) are portalled into the titlebar via a `[data-inspector-tabs-mount]` slot in `PanelChrome.tsx` and rendered as underlined tabs with 11px Ubuntu labels, a 2px purple bottom border on active, and a soft accent glow. Removes the duplicate "Properties" title beside the active tab and reclaims ~28px of vertical space in the docked and floating inspector. Fixes `assets/issues/08-properties-panel-header-single-row.md`; ships the user-selected "Underlined tabs" prototype from the three offered visual directions.

## v3.964.0 - 2026-07-20

- Docked Properties inspector, pass 3 (issue 07): identity collapsed into a single 24px row that fits name, `#N` int-alias, execution order, kind select, hide, and lock inline. Status strip is now an inline dot-list (Bounds ok / N conditions / Locked / Hidden) at 20px, no pills. Verdict bar (Accept / Reject / Ignore) uses check / x / minus icons with a filled active state (green / red / neutral) so the current verdict reads at a glance. Bounds aspect toggle becomes a 24px icon-only trailing button and the four inputs get equal columns. Acceptance empty state drops the dashed blue CTA for a ghost link. Mask, Focus, and Kind options are grouped under a single "More options" section that stays collapsed when empty, and the Kind sub-section header now uses the actual kind label (e.g. "Rect options"). Fixes 20 defects catalogued in `assets/issues/07-properties-panel-verdict-and-bounds-review.md`.

## v3.963.0 - 2026-07-20

- Docked Properties inspector, pass 2: identity row now surfaces the `#N` integer alias, execution `order N`, and inline lock / hide toggles so users no longer have to bounce to the Layers panel for basic metadata. A new status strip under the identity row summarizes bounds validity, condition count, locked, and hidden states as tone-coded chips. Bounds fields gain a per-input `px` suffix, drag-to-scrub on the X/Y/W/H labels, and an aspect-ratio lock button that keeps W/H proportional. The Acceptance section renders a single-line empty state (`Acceptance · none · + Add condition`) instead of a full card + paragraph, and adding a condition now seeds a fresh `present` condition in one click.

## v3.961.0 - 2026-07-20

- Editor top bar: added tiny inline `kbd` chips to Reset (⌘⇧0), Search (⌘K), Save (⌘S), and Publish (⌘⇧P) so power-user shortcuts are visible at a glance. Modifier auto-swaps to `Ctrl` on non-Mac. Chips hide under 900px and in Compact density to protect narrow layouts.

## v3.960.0 - 2026-07-20

- Editor top bar: grouped Reset, Search, and Save into a single segmented pill with hairline dividers. Publish stays standalone as the primary CTA. Segment buttons share one 28px height, 14px icons, and a subtle hover fill; Save turns primary-colored when the document is dirty.

## v3.959.0 - 2026-07-20

- Fixed the first-render Rules title so the panel opens as `Layers` immediately, then continues to follow Preview, Layers, or Properties tab changes. Browser verification on `/setup/roi` confirms a 52px left dock, 50px tool ribbon, 4px tile gap, no visible tool captions, no nested Properties action buttons, and a real `overflow-y: auto` inspector tab body.

## v3.958.0 - 2026-07-20

- Removed the remaining source-level causes behind the screenshots: Properties nested sections no longer render eye or close action buttons at all, Bounds uses a clean 12px internal pad with integer number fields, the Rules panel title now follows the active tab, the tab body has its own `min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto` scrollport, the right dock titlebar uses the same 12px gutter, and the left Tools rail is clamped through the panel, body, ribbon, and tile layers.

## v3.957.0 - 2026-07-20

- Fixed the remaining Tools rail regression by making the docked left rail physically fixed at 52px instead of only defaulting to 52px. The resize handle is disabled for the left dock, old persisted 90px widths are invalidated with layout persistence v8, the dock column and panel chrome are hard-clamped to 52px, and the ribbon now uses 36px icon-only tiles with 4px tile gaps and 8px group rhythm. Properties section chrome is also tightened in docked inspectors with hidden nested action icons, 24px section headers, and zero extra body padding.

## v3.956.0 - 2026-07-20

- Finished the missed density fixes from the screenshot critique. Tools is now a 52px dock with 36px icon-only tiles, no New/More captions, 4px tile rhythm, 8px grouped divider rhythm, and a 24px title strip with no close button in the docked rail. Rules tab content is now a real min-h-0 flex column with its own scrollport and the compact density gutter keeps rule rows aligned at 12px. Properties keeps search collapsed behind the icon, hides nested eye/close chrome in the inspector, rounds bounds to integer pixels, uses 28px identity controls, applies 12px section padding, and reduces the empty Acceptance state to a single split Add condition control.

## v3.955.0 - 2026-07-20

- Armed tool is now unmistakable at a glance. `.editor-tool-tile-selected` gains a 3px accent stripe on the leading edge (left in the vertical rail, bottom in top/bottom-docked ribbons) plus a stronger tint (`ca-primary` 22%), a `ca-primary` inset/outset ring, and a soft primary drop-shadow. Added `position: relative; overflow: hidden` to `.editor-tool-tile` so the `::before` stripe anchors cleanly without shifting layout.

## v3.954.0 - 2026-07-20

- Left tools rail now feels grouped and more compact. Reduced inter-tile vertical gap from `--spacing-hmi-1` to 2px on `.editor-tool-ribbon`, and inserted subtle hairline group dividers between the three logical clusters: Geometry (ROI, Rect) | Read (OCR, Text) | Compute (Math), plus a divider before the New/More action tiles. New CSS: `.editor-tool-group-divider` (1px gradient hairline, 8px side inset). Also closed a stray missing `}` on `.editor-tool-tile-caption` that was breaking the Tailwind CSS build.

## v3.953.0 - 2026-07-20

- The `+` (New) and `...` (More) tool tiles in the left ribbon now carry always-visible mini captions ("New" / "More") beneath the icon in addition to their existing tooltips. Tooltip labels tightened too: `label="New"` -> `"New rule"` and `label="More"` -> `"More tools"` so the native `title` + `aria-label` also read clearly. New CSS: `.editor-tool-tile-stack` (icon+caption column wrapper) and `.editor-tool-tile-caption` (9px uppercase muted label).

## v3.952.0 - 2026-07-20

- Tools rail title no longer truncates to "T..". Bumped left dock default width from 116px to 136px and hid the redundant `.panel-chrome-grip` inside the left dock (the whole titlebar is already the drag handle), reclaiming ~12px for the label. "Tools" now renders in full at default density while chevron + close controls stay put.

## v3.951.0 - 2026-07-20

- Toolbar tile tooltips completed. The 5 rule-kind tiles and the "New" tile already showed a tooltip with name + shortcut kbd + one-line description via `RibbonChip`; the "More" tile was the only one missing one and now shows "More tools" with an `M` kbd and a one-line description of what the popover contains. Also stripped the redundant "Press N." tail from every rule-kind description since the shortcut is already rendered as a `kbd` chip beside the name, so the copy no longer says the same thing twice.

## v3.950.0 - 2026-07-20

- Rule-row action buttons made compact and container-aware. Default icon button dropped 22x22 -> 20x20 with 12px glyphs (was 13px), gap unchanged at 1px so the cluster stays touchable but no longer dominates narrow rows. Added `container-type: inline-size` to `.editor-rule-row` and a `@container (max-width: 260px)` rule that further shrinks buttons to 18x18 with 11px glyphs when the row is narrow (floating Rules windows, split rails, mobile). Rows now stay aligned across widths instead of the last action wrapping or the name truncating early.

## v3.949.0 - 2026-07-20

- Floating Rules panel densified. Position+Size merged into one 4-column Bounds row; Name+Kind collapsed onto a single header line; per-section eye/close buttons hidden inside floating windows (redundant with the window frame); inspector search/chip strip/inputs tightened.

## v3.948.0 - 2026-07-20

- Editor top bar buttons compacted. Height dropped from 44px to 28px, padding tightened to 0 10px, icons scaled 18->14, font stepped to caption size and weight 600. Reset layout, Search, Save, and Publish now form a slim single-line toolbar instead of chunky tap targets that dwarfed the "Saved" pill.

## v3.947.0 - 2026-07-20

- Rules/Layers chrome cut down honestly. Removed the "N layers" RulesRailHeader strip and the "Inspector" CollapsiblePanelSection wrapper so the panel now stacks only: outer title -> tab bar -> LayersToolbar -> sticky section head -> rows (down from 5 chrome bands to 3). Dropped the redundant "N selected" chip from LayersToolbar (row highlight already conveys selection). Selected rows no longer force the action cluster visible, so on narrow rails the name span reclaims that horizontal space and stops truncating to "U1..." / "Rig..."; actions still reveal on hover, focus-within, or keyboard focus.

## v3.946.0 - 2026-07-20

- Rules/Layers panel cleanup. Removed the redundant "Layers · Run order N" strip that stacked between the panel title and the sticky Rules/Categories section heads (dropping the header count from 5 to 2). Fixed the aggressive name truncation on rule rows: the name span now takes `min-w-0 flex-1` and the source-chain badge + `ValidationChip` are `shrink-0`, so names fill the full available width and truncate only when they actually need to. Shrunk the execution-order badge to 18x16px so it steals less horizontal space from the name. The empty Categories state collapsed from a full bordered filler to a single 20px "None." line so the panel does not waste space when a rule set has no categories.

## v3.945.0 - 2026-07-20

- Tools panel is now truly responsive at very narrow widths. The vertical ribbon gets `min-width: 44px`, vertical scroll, `overflow-x: hidden`, and a container query at `<= 52px` that drops side padding to zero and tightens the gap so the 40px icon tiles never clip or overlap when the rail is squeezed. The horizontal dock variant now uses `flex-wrap: wrap` so top/bottom tool ribbons wrap onto a second row instead of clipping trailing tiles. Tooltips already surface the tool label in icon-only mode via `ToolTile` aria-label + native title.

## v3.944.0 - 2026-07-20

- Tools palette tiles: selected and unselected states now share identical layout, sizing, and elevation. Selection is expressed only through the border color and background tint (no more elevation-4 drop shadow on the active tile), so switching tools no longer visually shifts or "pops" the tile against its neighbors.

## v3.943.0 - 2026-07-20

- Fix the missed Tools and Inspector cleanup. `ToolRibbon` now renders the More control as the same compact 40px icon-only tile as the other tools, and the tools palette CSS removes oversized gaps plus hover translation so the palette does not jump. `PanelChrome` hover no longer shifts controls, and the global button / `ca-lift` hover rules now animate shadow and color without moving position. `LayerRow` moves Visibility / Lock / Delete / Edit into one right-aligned action cluster, replaces the ad-hoc order badge with `editor-rule-order-badge`, shrinks icon/kind badge sizing, and softens selected row chrome. `LayersPanel` and `InspectorSurface` now use dedicated CSS hooks for the Layers header, Rules/Categories sticky headers, group rows, and Inspector tabs so tab padding and section baselines line up instead of relying on scattered utility classes. Package and README pin advanced to v3.943.0.

## v3.942.0 - 2026-07-20

- Extend UI density (`useUiPrefsStore.headerDensity`) beyond the right rail so Compact / Comfortable now governs the entire editor shell. `src/components/editor/shell/EditorShell.tsx` reads the preference and stamps `data-density` on the `.editor-shell` root, so the cascade covers the left tools rail (`[data-testid="tools-palette"]`), the editor top bar (`.editor-shell-topbar`, `.editor-topbar-button`, `.editor-save-pill`), and any docked rail already inside. `src/components/app-shell/panels/FloatingWindow.tsx` also mirrors `data-density` onto every `.panel-floating-window` portal so torn-out panels stay in sync when dragged onto a second monitor (portal escapes the `.editor-shell` subtree). New `src/styles.css` block adds Compact overrides: tools rail 32px wide with 28x28 tiles + 14px svgs, top bar height 44px with 26px buttons and tighter save pill, floating window chrome shares the same tightened rail-panel head padding + 16x16 icon buttons. Comfortable is untouched, so existing screens stay pixel-stable.

## v3.941.0 - 2026-07-20

- Extend the existing `useUiPrefsStore.headerDensity` preference ("comfortable" | "compact") from a titlebar-only knob into a unified right-rail density switch. `src/components/editor/rail/RightRail.tsx` now reads `headerDensity` and stamps `data-density={density}` plus `className="right-rail"` on the `<aside>` root (also adds `data-testid="right-rail"` for tests). `src/styles.css` gets a new scoped block keyed on `.right-rail[data-density="compact"]` that shrinks: `.rail-panel-head` padding (`4px hmi-3` -> `2px hmi-2`), nested Properties sub-panel head padding, `.rail-panel-head-toggle` font-size/gap/padding, `.rail-panel-chevron` to 10x10, `.rail-panel-icon-button` to 16x16 with 10x10 svgs, `.rail-panel-head-actions` gap to 1px, `.rail-panel-body` font-size, `.editor-layers-toolbar` padding/gap, and `.editor-rule-icon` to 22x22 with 12x12 svgs. Comfortable mode is unchanged (no rule added), so existing screens do not shift. The Settings > UI density card description in `src/routes/settings.index.tsx` was rewritten to reflect the widened scope; the radio group and store contract are unchanged so the existing preview and persistence still work.

## v3.937.0 - 2026-07-20

- Enforce integer-only user-facing ids in the Titlebar address bar and collapse the duplicate header on the per-ruleset editor. Root cause: (a) `src/components/shell/AddressBar.tsx` still translated `projects/<uuid>` and `rulesets/<uuid>` segments to name slugs like `/projects/blister-pack-qa/rulesets/pill`, exposing internal ids the operator explicitly rejected; (b) `src/routes/projects.$projectId.rulesets.$rulesetId.tsx:494-583` rendered a large `font-display uppercase` H1 + subtitle + toolbar stack while the sticky Titlebar breadcrumb + address bar already carried the ruleset identity, so the workspace opened with three stacked identity bands before the canvas. Fixed: (a) AddressBar now rewrites `projects` / `rulesets` / `categories` / `trial-run` segments via `toIntParam` from `src/lib/ids/int-alias.ts` and `rules` segments via `toIntId` from `src/lib/rules/rule-id-alias.ts`; commit path resolves numeric segments back through `resolveIdParam` / `fromIntId` (legacy real-id paths still work), so bookmarks stay stable and the visible URL is always small positive integers. (b) Removed the redundant `<header>` block in the ruleset editor; rule-count moved inline as a muted chip on the toolbar row, and button padding was tightened to `px-hmi-2 py-hmi-1` / 14px icons for a single compact band. Rewrote `src/components/shell/__tests__/AddressBar.slug-roundtrip.test.tsx` to lock the integer round-trip (idle display, alias -> real-id commit, legacy-id passthrough, no-op equality). Tests: 9/9 pass. Typecheck: `bunx tsgo --noEmit` clean.

## v3.936.0 - 2026-07-20

- Fix SelectionOverlay quick-actions toolbar overlapping the X·Y / W×H numeric row, name chip, and rotate handle. Root cause: `SelectionOverlay.tsx:1547-1587` anchored the Duplicate/Delete strip at `left: br.x, top: tl.y - 32, translateX(-100%)`, placing it in the exact horizontal band already occupied by the two-row badge stack above the ROI. Redocked as a vertical strip to the OUTSIDE-RIGHT edge (`left: br.x + 8, top: tl.y`) with an auto-flip to outside-LEFT when `br.x + gap + width` exceeds `IMAGE_BOUNDS.width`, so buttons never render off-canvas and never collide with the badges/handle. No behavior changes: same actions, same test ids, same disabled/locked rules. Typecheck clean.

## v3.935.0 - 2026-07-20

- Extend deterministic integer-alias seeding to the generic `src/lib/ids/int-alias.ts` (Projects, Rulesets, Runs, Categories). Root cause: `toIntParam` still assigned integers lazily on first URL visit, so two users hydrating from the same persisted `ca:projects:v1` payload got different `/projects/N` numbers depending on click order. Added `seedIntParams(ns, readonly string[])` mirroring `seedIntIds`: dedupes, filters numeric-looking ids, sorts lexicographically, preserves any existing alias (URL bookmarks stable), and commits in one localStorage write. Wired into `useProjectStore`'s `persist` config via `onRehydrateStorage` (`src/lib/projects/store.ts:649-664`), which seeds both `project` and `ruleset` namespaces right after rehydrate before any component can request an alias. Rehydrate errors are logged and the seed step short-circuits (no throw). Added 4 tests in `src/lib/ids/__tests__/int-alias.test.ts` covering lex-sorted assignment, cross-install determinism, prior-alias preservation, and numeric-id passthrough. Tests: 4/4 pass under jsdom.

## v3.934.0 - 2026-07-20

- Deterministic integer-alias seeding during rule-facade hydration. Root cause: `toIntId` assigned integers lazily in the order URLs were first visited, so two users (or two browsers) seeded from the same `bundle.v2.json` would see different `/setup/rules/N` numbers for the same rule. Added `seedIntIds(readonly string[])` in `src/lib/rules/rule-id-alias.ts`: sorts input ids lexicographically (byte-order, locale-independent), skips any id already mapped (preserving bookmarked URLs), and writes the batch in a single localStorage round-trip. Wired into `IndexedDbRuleFacade.ensureHydrated` (`src/lib/rules/facade.ts:89-99`) so the map's keys are seeded once, right after the persisted rows are parsed, before any subscriber can request an alias. Same seed -> same integers on every browser and every user. Added 3 new tests in `src/lib/rules/__tests__/rule-id-alias.test.ts` covering sorted assignment, idempotent re-seed with preserved prior aliases, and two-fresh-install determinism. Typecheck: `bunx tsgo --noEmit` clean. Tests: 8/8 pass.

## v3.933.0 - 2026-07-20

- Fix rule-id leak in `/setup/rules/$id`. Root cause: `setup.rules.tsx:368` (post-create navigate), `setup.rules.tsx:672` (`RuleRow` Link target), `setup.categories.$id.tsx:48` (wrong-surface bounce), and `diagnostics.tsx:289` (audit-row Link) all passed the raw `Rule.id` (uuid/slug) into the `$id` param instead of the integer alias, so URLs like `/setup/rules/rule-adeb7e88-...` leaked internal ids into the address bar. All four sites now wrap the id in `toIntId(String(...))` from `src/lib/rules/rule-id-alias.ts`, matching the projects/rulesets nested rule flow. `src/routes/setup.rules.$id.tsx` now reverses numeric segments via `fromIntId(Number(id))` before calling `byId(...)`, with a raw-id fallback so old bookmarks still resolve. Typecheck: `bunx tsgo --noEmit` clean.

## v3.925.0 - 2026-07-20

- Right rail can now shrink further AND Properties gained more subdivisions (user screenshot 2026-07-20). Root cause: (a) `src/lib/workspace/layout-slice.ts:49` clamped every non-top / non-left dock at `DOCK_SIZE_MIN = 200`, so the right rail could not narrow past 200px even though `DockResizeHandle` in `src/components/app-shell/panels/DockSlot.tsx:44-101` already wired live resize + persistence; (b) Step 7's Bounds section was still one `CollapsiblePanelSection` with all four fields (X/Y/W/H), so operators had no way to hide position while keeping size visible or vice versa. Fix (minimum tied to cause): (1) added `DOCK_RIGHT_SIZE_MIN = 160` constant and routed `dockMinSize("right")` to it, so the right dock now clamps at 160px (still fits a `NumberInput` row plus the `.rail-panel-head` chevron gutter). Left/top/bottom minima unchanged. `clampDockSize` inherits the new floor because it calls `dockMinSize`, so persisted-then-migrated snapshots also get the wider range. (2) Split the "bounds" subsection in `src/components/editor/PropertiesPanel.tsx` into two independent `CollapsiblePanelSection`s with storage keys `properties.position` (X, Y) and `properties.size` (W, H); both default open. Kept the outer `data-inspector-section="bounds"` wrapper intact so the sticky chip strip's IntersectionObserver + inspector search filter continue to resolve as one section. `BoundsEditor` grew a `fields?: "all" | "position" | "size"` prop (default "all" for back-compat) that conditionally renders X/Y vs W/H; validator + patch path shared, so error highlighting and the "Fix the highlighted fields" banner still fire from the Position subsection (the source-of-truth for image dims). (3) Replaced the `properties.bounds` entry in `RAIL_PANELS` inside `src/components/app-shell/WindowMenu.tsx` with `properties.position` + `properties.size` so both subsections are restorable from the Window menu after being closed. No other consumers of the old `properties.bounds` storage key exist (grep clean). Pin advanced to v3.925.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.924.0 - 2026-07-20

- Plan 88 Step 6: nested rail-panel visual hierarchy in `src/styles.css` (`.rail-panel-body .rail-panel-section` scope). Root cause: Step 7 wrapped Bounds / Acceptance / Mask / Focus / Kind in `CollapsiblePanelSection`, but `.rail-panel-head` uses the same background tint, solid 1px border-bottom, and 600-weight caption at every nesting level, so the Properties parent and every subsection header rendered identically and the parent's closing border stacked against the first child's top border into a doubled seam. Fix (CSS-only, no component churn): (1) nested sections get a 600pacity border-bottom instead of full-weight, and `:last-child` drops the border entirely so the outer panel closes cleanly; (2) nested `.rail-panel-head` loses the tinted background, gains a dashed 550pacity separator when the section is open (solid muted line when collapsed) and an extra 8px left indent to signal hierarchy; (3) nested `.rail-panel-head-toggle` drops to weight 500 muted caption in uppercase with 0.06em tracking, brightening to `--ca-ink` only when the section is open. Parent-level `.rail-panel-section` (Preview / Layers / Properties in `InspectorSurface.tsx:144-209`) is untouched because the ancestor selector `.rail-panel-body .rail-panel-section` never matches a top-level panel. `prefers-reduced-motion` block is preserved. No JS/TSX edits. Pin advanced to v3.924.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.923.0 - 2026-07-20

- Plan 88 Step 7: `PropertiesPanel.tsx` migrates its five inspector subsections (Bounds, Acceptance, Mask, Focus, Kind) from plain `<div>` wrappers to `CollapsiblePanelSection` with namespaced storage keys `properties.bounds` / `properties.acceptance` / `properties.mask` / `properties.focus` / `properties.kind`. Root cause: operator asked for "several smaller sections which can be minimized, hidden, or crossed out" but only the outer Properties panel (Step 5) was collapsible, leaving each subsection as a static block. Fix (minimum tied to cause): wrap each of the five conditional section blocks with the primitive, keeping the outer `data-inspector-section` div intact so the sticky chip strip's IntersectionObserver and the inspector search filter still work; kept Bounds + Acceptance `defaultOpen`, Mask/Focus/Kind collapsed to preserve the current above-the-fold layout. Registered the five new keys in `RAIL_PANELS` inside `src/components/app-shell/WindowMenu.tsx` so closed subsections are restorable from the Window menu via `useRailPanelState` (same aliased `hideRailPanel`/`restoreRailPanel` path added in Step 8). No new styles required (the primitive already ships `.rail-panel-*`). Bundle unchanged for hidden subsections because the primitive keeps a `display:none` marker instead of unmounting, so chip navigation still resolves. Pin advanced to v3.923.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.922.0 - 2026-07-20

- Plan 88 Step 8 (pulled ahead of Step 6 because it delivers user-visible value; Step 6 is layout polish): `WindowMenu.tsx` gains a "Rail panels" section that restores hidden Preview / Layers / Properties panels. Root cause being fixed: Step 5 gave those three sections Hide (eye) and Close (X) icon buttons via `CollapsiblePanelSection`, but there was no UI path back — closing the Properties panel effectively removed it until the operator manually cleared `localStorage.hmi.rail.panel:inspector.properties.hidden`, making the controls one-way and violating the primitive's own header title "Close panel (restore from Window menu)". Fix (minimum tied to cause): (1) imported `useRailPanelState` from `@/hooks/useRailPanelState` in `src/components/app-shell/WindowMenu.tsx`; (2) added a top-of-file `RAIL_PANELS` constant listing the three known storageKeys (`inspector.preview`, `inspector.layers`, `inspector.properties`) with their titles so future rail panels only need one edit; (3) destructured the hook's methods with rename aliases (`isRailPanelHidden`, `hideRailPanel`, `restoreRailPanel`) to avoid a TS2451 collision with the existing `restorePanel` from `useWorkspaceLayoutStore` (that error surfaced during the first pass and was fixed before finishing); (4) inserted a new `DropdownMenuLabel "Rail panels"` block between the existing "Panels" list and the "Layout presets" section, rendering one `DropdownMenuItem` per rail panel with a `Check` when visible, an "Hidden" muted tag when hidden, and an `onSelect` that flips state via `restoreRailPanel` / `hideRailPanel` — both dispatch `broadcastRailPanelHidden` which the `CollapsiblePanelSection` instance listens for, so the DOM updates without a store lift. Each item carries `data-testid="window-menu-rail-<key>"` for the Step 10 Playwright pass. Observability chain end to end: user clicks the menu item → `restoreRailPanel(key)` → `broadcastRailPanelHidden(key, false)` → primitive's window listener sets `hidden=false` → primitive's `useEffect` writes `"0"` to `hmi.rail.panel:<key>.hidden` AND re-emits the event → `useRailPanelState`'s listener drops the key from its Set → menu item's check re-appears → `logger.info("I_UI_RAIL_PANEL_SET_HIDDEN", {storageKey, hidden:false})` fires. Pin advanced to v3.922.0 in `README.md`.

- Plan 88 Step 5: Preview / Layers / Properties rail sections migrated from the plain `CollapsibleSection` (open/closed only) to the new `CollapsiblePanelSection` primitive (Step 3), which adds Hide (eye) and Close (X) icon controls on each section header. Root cause being unblocked: Step 4's `useRailPanelState` aggregator had nothing to aggregate because the three top-level rail sections in `src/components/editor/InspectorSurface.tsx:139-200` still rendered `CollapsibleSection`, so operators couldn't hide/close them and `WindowMenu` (Step 8) would find an empty hidden set. Fix (minimum tied to cause): swapped the three wrappers to `CollapsiblePanelSection` with namespaced storage keys `inspector.preview`, `inspector.layers`, `inspector.properties` (namespaced so they don't collide with the legacy `storageKey="preview"|"layers"|"properties"` used by inner subsections and by `PropertiesPanel`'s Transform/Appearance/… children), preserved existing `defaultOpen` values (preview=false, layers=true, properties=false), and updated the import to `./rail/CollapsiblePanelSection`. Styles are already present in `src/styles.css` (`.rail-panel-section`, `.rail-panel-head`, `.rail-panel-body` shipped in Step 3, verified via `grep -c` = 7 matches). No behavior regression: `broadcastInspectorSections()` (used by RulesRailHeader's Expand-all/Collapse-all menu) still targets the inner `CollapsibleSection` subsections inside PropertiesPanel, not the top-level panels, so the ⋯ menu semantics are unchanged. Bundle unchanged for hidden panels because `CollapsiblePanelSection` renders a `display:none` marker `[data-rail-panel-hidden="true"]` instead of unmounting, which Step 8's WindowMenu will enumerate together with the localStorage set. Pin advanced to v3.921.0 in `README.md`.

- Plan 88 Step 4: `useRailPanelState` hook added at `src/hooks/useRailPanelState.ts`. Root cause being unblocked: each `CollapsiblePanelSection` (Step 3) owns its own hidden flag and mirrors it to `hmi.rail.panel:<key>.hidden` in localStorage, so `WindowMenu` (Step 8), keyboard shortcuts, and telemetry had no single source of truth for "which rail panels are currently hidden"; scanning the DOM is racy during hydration and duplicating the storage-key format would drift the moment the primitive changes. Fix (minimum tied to the cause): a dedicated hook that (a) seeds a `Set<string>` of hidden storageKeys by scanning `window.localStorage` for `hmi.rail.panel:*.hidden === "1"` after mount (SSR-safe: initial state is an empty set so server and first client render match, then `useEffect` hydrates from storage), (b) subscribes to the primitive's `RAIL_PANEL_SET_HIDDEN_EVENT` CustomEvent so intra-tab changes propagate without polling, (c) subscribes to the browser `storage` event so cross-tab changes propagate too, and (d) exposes `hiddenPanels`, `isHidden(key)`, `hidePanel(key)`, `restorePanel(key)`, `setPanelHidden(key, hidden)` where the mutators dispatch `broadcastRailPanelHidden` (imported from the primitive) so callers never touch localStorage directly and the primitive's own listener writes the storage entry, keeping one owner per key. Errors are logged via the shared editor logger with `W_UI_RAIL_PANEL_STATE_READ_FAILED` and `W_UI_RAIL_PANEL_STATE_EMPTY_KEY`, and every mutation emits `I_UI_RAIL_PANEL_SET_HIDDEN {storageKey, hidden}`, satisfying the project's no-silent-failure rule. Not yet consumed (Step 8 wires it into `WindowMenu`); build stays green because the hook is unimported. Pin advanced to v3.920.0 in `README.md`.

- Rules rail redesigned to the Compact CAD Toolbar theme (user pick). Root causes fixed: (a) the panel showed the word "Rules" twice, once from the outer window chrome and again as a large `editor-rail-header` H1 with a badge, plus a third giant "RULES" text row inside the IO bar; (b) Export / Import were stealing a whole 40px tall button row and Expand-all / Collapse-all were stealing a second row, together eating ~110px of vertical space before the first section header; (c) collapsible section titles sat on `padding: var(--spacing-hmi-2) var(--spacing-hmi-3)` at `text-hmi-body`, so the header block felt like a separate island rather than a header for the content directly below it. Fix: (1) new `src/components/editor/rail/RulesRailHeader.tsx` replaces `RuleSetIOBar` at the top of the rail: a single 28px dense row with a monospace "N layers" count on the left (`.rules-rail-count`, `font-hmi-mono`, uppercase caption size) and a 22x22 `MoreHorizontal` icon-only `⋯` trigger on the right that opens a shadcn `DropdownMenu` containing Export rule set / Import rule set / Expand all sections / Collapse all sections, with a separator between IO and section actions. (2) `src/components/editor/rail/RightRail.tsx` deleted the redundant `<div className="editor-rail-header"><span>Rules</span>…` block and swapped `RuleSetIOBar` for `RulesRailHeader`, killing the duplicate "Rules"/"RULES"/"4 active layers" triple-header. (3) `src/components/editor/InspectorSurface.tsx` removed the standalone `inspector-section-toolbar` (Expand all / Collapse all buttons); the same actions now live in the ⋯ menu, so the pane opens straight into Preview / Layers / Properties. (4) `src/styles.css` tightened `.inspector-section-head` from `padding: var(--spacing-hmi-2) var(--spacing-hmi-3)` at `text-hmi-body` to `padding: 6px var(--spacing-hmi-2)` at `text-hmi-caption` with `letter-spacing: 0.06em`, so titles align visually with the panel body's left edge and read as headers, not islands. New `.rules-rail-toolbar`, `.rules-rail-count`, and `.rules-rail-menu-trigger` styles added; legacy `.inspector-section-toolbar*` classes retained for the palette command surface. `RuleSetIOBar.tsx` left in place unused (no other importers) in case a future theme needs it. Properties panel already ships an inline search field (`InspectorSearch`, `data-testid="inspector-search"`) so no additional wiring was needed to satisfy the user's search request. Pin advanced to v3.919.0 in `README.md`.

- Plan 88 Step 3: Right-rail collapsible panel primitive `CollapsiblePanelSection` added at `src/components/editor/rail/CollapsiblePanelSection.tsx`. Root cause being unblocked: the existing `CollapsibleSection` in `src/components/editor/CollapsibleSection.tsx` only supports open/closed toggling for inspector subsections; the right rail (Preview / Layers / Properties) needs the operator to be able to hide or fully close a panel and later restore it from the Window menu, which requires (a) a second persisted flag ("hidden"), (b) icon controls on the header (chevron, eye, close), and (c) a broadcast event so `WindowMenu.tsx` in Step 8 can restore hidden panels without lifting state into a global store. Fix: new primitive persists both `hmi.rail.panel:<key>.open` and `hmi.rail.panel:<key>.hidden` to localStorage with deterministic SSR-safe initial state (open=defaultOpen, hidden=false, hydrated preferences applied in a post-mount effect, matching the pattern proven in `CollapsibleSection`), emits `hmi:rail-panel:set-hidden` CustomEvent on hidden-flag changes, and listens for the same event scoped by `storageKey` so external callers (WindowMenu, keyboard shortcuts) can restore a panel. Header renders a chevron+title toggle (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`), a hide/show icon button, and a close (X) button, all lucide-react icons at 12px matching existing rail density. Body is wrapped in `role="region"` with the panel title as `aria-label`. When `hidden=true` the section renders a display:none marker (`data-rail-panel-hidden="true"`) instead of unmounting, so tests and WindowMenu can enumerate hidden panels via `document.querySelectorAll('[data-rail-panel-hidden="true"]')`. Styles added to `src/styles.css` (`.rail-panel-section`, `.rail-panel-head`, `.rail-panel-head-toggle`, `.rail-panel-chevron`, `.rail-panel-title`, `.rail-panel-head-actions`, `.rail-panel-icon-button`, `.rail-panel-body`) with 160ms ease transitions for the chevron rotation, icon-button hover, and body reveal, all disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`. No callers migrated in this step (Steps 5-6 wire it into `RightRail.tsx`); build remains green because the new file is not yet imported anywhere. Pin advanced to v3.918.0 in `README.md`.

- Plan 88 Step 2: `StatusBar` promoted to a semantic fixed footer with a reserved gutter token. Root cause: the footer was only wired into `HmiShell`'s own flex column, so any overlay rendered outside that column (floating panels, toasts, and the collapsible right-rail primitives coming in Steps 3-8) had no way to reason about its height and would slide under it; separately, the whole `<footer>` was `role="status" aria-live="polite"`, so every re-render (program name change, prop churn) re-announced the entire footer instead of just run-state changes. Fix: (1) `src/components/hmi/HmiShell.tsx` now sets `--status-bar-h-effective` on the shell root via inline style, resolving to `var(--status-bar-h)` when `showStatusBar` is true and `0px` when the operator has hidden the bar via View -> Toggle Status Bar (Ctrl+/); descendants can now write `bottom: var(--status-bar-h-effective)` without measuring the DOM. Added `data-testid="hmi-shell"` for the same. (2) `src/components/hmi/StatusBar.tsx` now uses `role="contentinfo"` with `aria-label="Machine status"` and scopes the live region to an inner `<span role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">` that wraps only the RUNNING/IDLE + Total/OK/NG counters, so screen readers announce state changes, not the whole footer. Added `data-testid="hmi-status-bar"` and per-span `aria-label`s ("Active program", "Run status", "Run counters"). Verified in the live preview at `/setup/roi`: `footerRole="contentinfo"`, `aria-label="Machine status"`, exactly one inner `role="status"` node, `--status-bar-h-effective="2rem"`, footer height 31.99px, and `atBottom=true` (footer bottom equals `window.innerHeight`). Pin advanced to v3.917.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.916.0 - 2026-07-20

- Plan 88 Step 1: Home scroll + status bar chain fix. Root cause: `<main>` in `src/components/hmi/HmiShell.tsx` and the inner wrapper `<div>` in `src/routes/index.tsx` used `flex-1` inside a `flex-col` parent without `min-h-0`, so per the CSS flex spec their auto min content-size let the scroll region grow to intrinsic height, `overflow-auto` never clipped, keyboard scroll did nothing, and the `StatusBar` got pushed under the fold on short viewports. Fix (minimum, tied to root cause): added `min-h-0` to both flex ancestors so `overflow-auto` on the Home scroll region actually clips at the viewport. Also introduced `--status-bar-h: 2rem` in `src/styles.css` and switched `src/components/hmi/StatusBar.tsx` from the hardcoded `h-8` Tailwind class to `style={{ height: "var(--status-bar-h)" }}`, giving Step 2 (fixed-footer promotion) a single token to compute against without touching any other file. Verified in the running preview: the Home content scrolls with wheel, keyboard (ArrowDown / PageDown / End), and touchpad; the status bar stays pinned at the bottom of `HmiShell` and no longer overlaps content. Pin advanced to v3.916.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.915.0 - 2026-07-20

Twelve deterministic PNGs land in `tests/reports/home-regression/`. Extended `tests/e2e/diff_heatmaps.py` `SOURCES` to include the new directory: first run seeds baselines under `tests/reports/baselines/home-regression/`; later runs emit per-shot red-tinted heatmaps plus a side-by-side composite and fail the job when >20f pixels change (`THRESHOLD_RATIO = 0.02`, `PIXEL_DELTA = 12`). Wired both scripts into `.github/workflows/ci.yml` between `item_rows_long_labels.py` and the dev-server stop step, and added `tests/reports/home-regression/**` to the failure-artifact upload path so regressions are inspectable in the run summary. Verified end-to-end: seeded 12 baselines then re-ran capture+diff to confirm a clean 0-failure pass. This catches the two regressions the user hit twice this session (hero mt-hmi-5/mt-hmi-10 collapsing to 0, and object-cover stretching the reference photo) before they ship. Pin advanced to v3.915.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.914.0 - 2026-07-20

- Image aspect-ratio integrity pass: no user photo is stretched or cropped anywhere. Root cause: (1) `drawReference` in `src/lib/editor/render/frame.ts` called `ctx.drawImage(ref, x, y, IMAGE_BOUNDS.width, IMAGE_BOUNDS.height)`, forcing every uploaded/sample photo into the fixed 1280x720 editor viewport regardless of source ratio (this is the "stressed" PCB the user reported); (2) ten DOM image previews used `object-cover` which crops the frame on any non-matching ratio. Fix (canvas): switched `drawReference` to a contain-fit that scales the source by `Math.min(w/srcW, h/srcH)`, centers it inside `IMAGE_BOUNDS`, and letterboxes/pillarboxes the remainder with `tokens.panel`, so the photo keeps its native ratio and the coordinate space stays 1280x720 for rules. Fix (DOM): replaced `object-cover` with `object-contain bg-ca-panel` in `src/components/settings/ReferenceImageCard.tsx` (main preview, history thumbs, sample tiles), `src/components/projects/SampleCarousel.tsx` (chip + preview), `src/components/projects/ProjectEditorSections.tsx` (cover), `src/routes/projects.$projectId.ai-testing.tsx` (row thumb), `src/components/hmi/ViewportImageControls.tsx` (chip thumb), and `src/components/hmi/CameraPreview.tsx` (live view). `src/components/hmi/MachineFrame.tsx` was intentionally left as-is because it is a decorative background overlay (`opacity-70`), not a user photo. Pin advanced to v3.914.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.913.0 - 2026-07-20

- Top menu (home nav) hover stability + padding pass: eliminated hover jitter and normalized item padding across all `MenubarTrigger` items in `src/components/nav/TopMenuBar.tsx`. Root causes: (1) the trigger used `focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2` which layered an outside outline that visually nudged neighboring items on keyboard focus transitions; (2) trigger height was `h-7` inside an `h-7` bar leaving no visual breathing room and encouraging perceived shift; (3) `overflow-x-auto` on the `Menubar` allowed a phantom horizontal scroll to appear mid-hover on borderline widths; (4) padding was inconsistent (`px-1.5` on nav triggers, `px-1.5` + `mr-1` icon spacing on Window trigger). Fix: standardized all triggers to `h-6 px-hmi-2 leading-none` with `gap-1` for the icon+label Window trigger, removed the offset outline in favor of the existing inset `hmi-focus-ring-inset` (no layout impact), swapped `overflow-x-auto` -> `overflow-hidden` on the bar, gave the bar `gap-0.5 p-0.5` for even inter-item rhythm, and pinned `transition-colors duration-150 ease-out` so the color swap on hover/open is smooth without touching box metrics. No component structure or behavior changed; only visual polish. Pin advanced to v3.913.0 in `README.md`.

## v3.908.0 - 2026-07-20

- UI fix: permanent teal outlines on Home CTA + hero utility chips (Shortcuts / Density & theme / All projects) and every other element opting into the focus-ring utilities. Root cause: the `hmi-focus-ring`, `ca-focus-ring`, and `ca-focus-ring-inset` `@utility` blocks in `src/styles.css` (lines 419-440) set `outline: 2px solid var(--ca-focus-ring)` unconditionally, so any element that added the class rendered a bright cyan outline at all times, not just on keyboard focus, reading as a broken/stuck focus state (visible on Home hero: primary CTA + all three utility chips, plus many buttons/links across the app). Fix: gated the `outline` declaration inside a nested `&:focus-visible` selector in all three utilities, kept `border-radius: 4px` on the container-level `ca-focus-ring` / `ca-focus-ring-inset` so the ring shape still matches when it does appear, and preserved the existing global `:where(a, button, ...):focus-visible` fallback + `html[data-input-modality="keyboard"]` escalation at the bottom of the file. Verified via Playwright screenshot of `/` at 1280x900: Home CTA and the three utility chips render without any outline, hover-lift and text styles preserved, only the intended chip border remains. Pin advanced to v3.908.0 in `README.md`.
